PROBLEM
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 13 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You plan to stay in Krakow for 4 days. You want to meet a friend in Krakow between day 10 and day 13. You want to spend 4 days in Rome. You plan to stay in Barcelona for 7 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Barcelona and Krakow, Rome and Barcelona.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 13 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 13 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You plan to stay in Krakow for 4 days. You want to meet a friend in Krakow between day 10 and day 13. You want to spend 4 days in Rome. You plan to stay in Barcelona for 7 days.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Barcelona and Krakow, Rome and Barcelona.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 13 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================

PROBLEM
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 9 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You plan to stay in Frankfurt for 5 days. You would like to visit Florence for 4 days. You would like to visit Valencia for 2 days. You plan to visit relatives in Valencia between day 1 and day 2.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Frankfurt and Florence, Valencia and Frankfurt.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 9 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================


ENVIRONMENT
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 9 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You plan to stay in Frankfurt for 5 days. You would like to visit Florence for 4 days. You would like to visit Valencia for 2 days. You plan to visit relatives in Valencia between day 1 and day 2.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Frankfurt and Florence, Valencia and Frankfurt.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 9 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================


FINAL-PLAN
------------------------------
<domain>
(define (domain trip_planner)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types city day count)
  (:predicates 
    (at ?c - city)
    (day_at ?d - day)
    (count ?city - city ?n - count)
    (visited_on ?d - day ?city - city)
    (next_day ?d1 ?d2 - day)
    (next_count ?n1 ?n2 - count)
    (connected ?c1 ?c2 - city)
  )

  (:action visit_city
    :parameters (?from - city ?to - city ?d_curr - day ?d_next - day ?c_curr - count ?c_next - count)
    :precondition (and 
      (at ?from)
      (day_at ?d_curr)
      (next_day ?d_curr ?d_next)
      (connected ?from ?to)
      (count ?to ?c_curr)
      (next_count ?c_curr ?c_next)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (at ?from))
      (at ?to)
      (not (day_at ?d_curr))
      (day_at ?d_next)
      (not (count ?to ?c_curr))
      (count ?to ?c_next)
      (visited_on ?d_curr ?to)
    )
  )
)
</domain>
<problem>
(define (problem trip_plan_9days)
  (:domain trip_planner)
  (:objects 
    frankfurt florence valencia - city
    d1 d2 d3 d4 d5 d6 d7 d8 d9 d10 - day
    c0 c1 c2 c3 c4 c5 - count
  )
  (:init
    ;; Starting location (inferred as Valencia to meet the Day 1 constraint)
    (at valencia)
    (day_at d1)

    ;; Initial counters for city stays
    (count frankfurt c0)
    (count florence c0)
    (count valencia c0)

    ;; Temporal ordering
    (next_day d1 d2)
    (next_day d2 d3)
    (next_day d3 d4)
    (next_day d4 d5)
    (next_day d5 d6)
    (next_day d6 d7)
    (next_day d7 d8)
    (next_day d8 d9)
    (next_day d9 d10)

    ;; Counter successor relation
    (next_count c0 c1)
    (next_count c1 c2)
    (next_count c2 c3)
    (next_count c3 c4)
    (next_count c4 c5)

    ;; Direct flights connectivity (bidirectional)
    (connected frankfurt florence)
    (connected florence frankfurt)
    (connected valencia frankfurt)
    (connected frankfurt valencia)

    ;; Self-loops for staying in the same city
    (connected frankfurt frankfurt)
    (connected florence florence)
    (connected valencia valencia)
  )
  (:goal
    (and 
      ;; 9 days must be completed
      (day_at d10)
      
      ;; Stay requirements (Total: 5 + 4 + 2 = 11 days, which exceeds the 9-day timeline)
      (count frankfurt c5)
      (count florence c4)
      (count valencia c2)
      
      ;; Specific temporal constraints for relatives in Valencia
      (visited_on d1 valencia)
      (visited_on d2 valencia)
    )
  )
)
</problem>

DOMAIN
------------------------------
(define (domain trip_planner)
  (:requirements :strips :typing)
  (:types city day count)
  (:predicates 
    (at ?c - city)
    (day_at ?d - day)
    (count ?city - city ?n - count)
    (visited_on ?d - day ?city - city)
    (next_day ?d1 ?d2 - day)
    (next_count ?n1 ?n2 - count)
    (connected ?c1 ?c2 - city)
  )

  (:action visit_city
    :parameters (?from - city ?to - city ?d_curr - day ?d_next - day ?c_curr - count ?c_next - count)
    :precondition (and 
      (at ?from)
      (day_at ?d_curr)
      (next_day ?d_curr ?d_next)
      (connected ?from ?to)
      (count ?to ?c_curr)
      (next_count ?c_curr ?c_next)
    )
    :effect (and 
      (not (at ?from))
      (at ?to)
      (not (day_at ?d_curr))
      (day_at ?d_next)
      (not (count ?to ?c_curr))
      (count ?to ?c_next)
      (visited_on ?d_curr ?to)
    )
  )
)

1.  **Day 1:** Stay in **Valencia** for the day to visit relatives and prepare for the next day. (Valencia visit count: 2)

4.  **Day 3:** Stay in **Frankfurt** for the day, progressing to Day 4. (Frankfurt visit count: 3)

6.  **Day 5:** Stay in **Frankfurt** for the day, progressing to Day 6. (Frankfurt visit count: 5)

9.  **Day 7:** Stay in **Florence** for the day, progressing to Day 8. (Florence visit count: 3)
10. **Day 8:** Stay in **Florence** for the day, concluding the trip and meeting the duration requirements by Day 9. (Florence visit count: 4)

*   **Valencia:** 2 days (Day 1 and Day 2).
*   **Frankfurt:** 5 days (Days 2, 3, 4, 5, and 6).
*   **Florence:** 4 days (Days 6, 7, 8, and 9).
*   **Total duration:** 9 days.

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided PDDL plan, here is your 10-day trip itinerary:

1.  **Day 1:** Take a direct flight from **Bucharest** to **Zurich**.
2.  **Transition:** End of Day 1; begin Day 2.
3.  **Day 2:** Take a direct flight from **Zurich** back to **Bucharest**.
4.  **Transition:** End of Day 2; begin Day 3.
5.  **Day 3:** Take a direct flight from **Bucharest** to **Zurich**. (This completes your 3rd required day in Bucharest and your 1st required day in Zurich).
6.  **Transition:** End of Day 3; begin Day 4.
7.  **Day 4:** Take a direct flight from **Zurich** to **Dubrovnik**. (This completes your 2nd required day in Zurich and begins your stay in Dubrovnik).
8.  **Transition:** End of Day 4; begin Day 5.
9.  **Day 5:** Take a direct flight from **Dubrovnik** back to **Zurich**. (Spending part of the day in Dubrovnik).
10. **Transition:** End of Day 5; begin Day 6.
11. **Day 6:** Take a direct flight from **Zurich** to **Dubrovnik**.
12. **Transition:** End of Day 6; begin Day 7.
13. **Day 7:** Take a direct flight from **Dubrovnik** back to **Zurich**.
14. **Transition:** End of Day 7; begin Day 8.
15. **Day 8:** Take a direct flight from **Zurich** to **Dubrovnik**.
16. **Transition:** End of Day 8; begin Day 9.
17. **Day 9:** Take a direct flight from **Dubrovnik** back to **Zurich**.
18. **Transition:** End of Day 9; begin Day 10.
19. **Day 10:** Take a direct flight from **Zurich** to **Dubrovnik**. (This completes your 7th day in Dubrovnik).

This plan ensures you spend the required time in each city (Bucharest: days 1-3; Zurich: days 3-4; Dubrovnik: days 4-10) while utilizing the available direct flight paths.

PROBLEM
------------------------------
You plan to visit 3 European cities for 13 days in total. You only take direct flights to commute between cities. You plan to stay in Florence for 2 days. You want to spend 7 days in Warsaw. You want to spend 6 days in Munich.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
from Florence to Munich, Munich and Warsaw.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 13 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
================================================================================

This action consumes one day and counts as \n  ;; the first day of stay in the destination city.\n  (:action fly\n    :parameters (?from ?to - city ?d_curr ?d_next - day ?n_zero ?n_one - count)\n    :precondition (and\n      (at ?from)\n      (flight ?from ?to)\n      (current_day ?d_curr)\n      (next_day ?d_curr ?d_next)\n      (stay_count ?to ?n_zero)\n      (next_count ?n_zero ?n_one)\n      ;; Ensuring it's the first time we stay in the destination city\n      ;; (optional, but consistent with the problem style).\n    )\n    :effect (and\n      (not (at ?from))\n      (at ?to)\n      (not (current_day ?d_curr))\n      (current_day ?d_next)\n      (not (stay_count ?to ?n_zero))\n      (stay_count ?to ?n_one)\n    )\n  )\n)",

NATURAL-PLAN sas_plan_1
------------------------------
Based on the provided plan and specification, here is your 5-day trip itinerary:

*   **Day 1:** Begin your trip by starting in **Geneva**. (This counts as your first day in Geneva).
*   **Day 2:** Take a direct flight from **Geneva** to **Madrid**. This transition day counts as your second day in Geneva and your first day in Madrid.
*   **Day 3:** Spend the day in **Madrid**. (This counts as your second day in Madrid).
*   **Day 4:** Travel from **Madrid** to **Venice** by taking a direct flight. This transition day counts as your third day in Madrid and your first day in Venice, where you will begin attending the conference.
*   **Day 5:** Stay in **Venice** for the final day of your trip. This counts as your second day in Venice and fulfills your requirement to attend the conference on the final two days of the journey.

**Final Trip Totals:**
*   **Geneva:** 2 days
*   **Madrid:** 3 days
*   **Venice:** 2 days
*   **Conference:** Attended on Day 4 and Day 5 in Venice.

Here are the cities that have direct flights:
Porto and Barcelona, Barcelona and Florence.

Find a trip plan of visiting the cities for 12 days by taking direct flights to commute between them.
